
===== FILE: src/hooks/use-community-stats.ts =====
'use client'

import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useSession } from 'next-auth/react'
import {
  EMPTY_COMMUNITY_STATS,
  type CommunityStats,
} from '@/lib/badge-meta'

interface CommunityProfilePayload {
  data?: {
    followers?: number
    createdAt?: string
    contributions?: {
      projects?: number
      comments?: number
      upvotes?: number
    }
  }
}

interface StoredCommunityStats {
  userId: string
  stats: CommunityStats
}

export interface CommunityStatsResult {
  stats: CommunityStats
  isLoaded: boolean
}

/**
 * Loads the signed-in learner's community counters so badge checks can use
 * projects, comments, upvotes, followers, and join date. Falls back to zeros
 * when signed out, loading, offline, or when community features are disabled.
 */
export function useCommunityStats(): CommunityStatsResult {
  const { data: session } = useSession()
  const userId = session?.user?.id
  const [stored, setStored] = useState<StoredCommunityStats | null>(null)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!userId) return

    const controller = new AbortController()

    fetch(`/api/community/people/${encodeURIComponent(userId)}`, {
      signal: controller.signal,
    })
      .then((response) => (response.ok ? response.json() : null))
      .then((payload: CommunityProfilePayload | null) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return
        const data = payload?.data

        setStored({
          userId,
          stats: data
            ? {
                followers: data.followers ?? 0,
                joinedAt: data.createdAt ?? null,
                contributions: {
                  projects: data.contributions?.projects ?? 0,
                  comments: data.contributions?.comments ?? 0,
                  upvotes: data.contributions?.upvotes ?? 0,
                },
              }
            : EMPTY_COMMUNITY_STATS,
        })
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return
        // Community features may be disabled; badges relying on them stay locked.
        setStored({ userId, stats: EMPTY_COMMUNITY_STATS })
      })

    return () => controller.abort()
  }, [userId])

  if (!userId) return { stats: EMPTY_COMMUNITY_STATS, isLoaded: true }
  if (stored?.userId !== userId) return { stats: EMPTY_COMMUNITY_STATS, isLoaded: false }
  return { stats: stored.stats, isLoaded: true }
}


===== FILE: src/hooks/use-progress.ts =====
'use client'

import { useState, useEffect, useCallback } from 'react'
import {
  defaultProgressState,
  getToday,
  loadProgressState,
  saveProgressState,
  type ProgressState,
} from '@/lib/progress-state'

export type { LessonProgress, ProgressState } from '@/lib/progress-state'

export function useProgress() {
  const [state, setState] = useState<ProgressState>(defaultProgressState)
  const [isLoaded, setIsLoaded] = useState(false)

  useEffect(() => {
    // Hydrate from localStorage (external store) on mount.
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/set-state-in-effect
    setState(loadProgressState())
    setIsLoaded(true)

    const handleUpdate = (e: Event) => {
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent).detail
      setState(detail)
    }
    window.addEventListener('progress-updated', handleUpdate)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('progress-updated', handleUpdate)
  }, [])

  const updateStreak = useCallback((current: ProgressState): ProgressState => {
    const today = getToday()
    const { lastActiveDate, current: currentStreak, longest } = current.streak

    if (lastActiveDate === today) return current

    const yesterday = new Date()
    yesterday.setDate(yesterday.getDate() - 1)
    const yesterdayStr = yesterday.toISOString().split('T')[0]

    const newCurrent = lastActiveDate === yesterdayStr ? currentStreak + 1 : 1
    const newLongest = Math.max(newCurrent, longest)

    return {
      ...current,
      streak: {
        current: newCurrent,
        longest: newLongest,
        lastActiveDate: today,
      },
    }
  }, [])

  const markComplete = useCallback((lessonId: string, xpReward: number, completed: boolean) => {
    setState((prev) => {
      const wasCompleted = prev.lessons[lessonId]?.completed ?? false
      let xpDelta = 0

      if (completed && !wasCompleted) xpDelta = xpReward
      else if (!completed && wasCompleted) xpDelta = -xpReward

      let next: ProgressState = {
        ...prev,
        lessons: {
          ...prev.lessons,
          [lessonId]: {
            ...prev.lessons[lessonId],
            completed,
            completedAt: completed ? new Date().toISOString() : undefined,
          },
        },
        xp: Math.max(0, prev.xp + xpDelta),
      }

      if (completed) {
        next = updateStreak(next)
      }

      saveProgressState(next)
      return next
    })
  }, [updateStreak])

  const saveNotes = useCallback((lessonId: string, notes: string) => {
    setState((prev) => {
      const next: ProgressState = {
        ...prev,
        lessons: {
          ...prev.lessons,
          [lessonId]: { ...prev.lessons[lessonId], completed: prev.lessons[lessonId]?.completed ?? false, notes },
        },
      }
      saveProgressState(next)
      return next
    })
  }, [])

  const toggleBookmark = useCallback((lessonId: string) => {
    setState((prev) => {
      const bookmarks = prev.bookmarks.includes(lessonId)
        ? prev.bookmarks.filter((id) => id !== lessonId)
        : [...prev.bookmarks, lessonId]
      const next = { ...prev, bookmarks }
      saveProgressState(next)
      return next
    })
  }, [])

  const setLastVisited = useCallback((track: string, module: string, lesson: string) => {
    setState((prev) => {
      const next: ProgressState = {
        ...prev,
        lastVisited: { track, module, lesson, timestamp: new Date().toISOString() },
      }
      saveProgressState(next)
      return next
    })
  }, [])

  const resetProgress = useCallback(() => {
    saveProgressState(defaultProgressState)
    setState(defaultProgressState)
  }, [])

  const isCompleted = useCallback((lessonId: string) => {
    return state.lessons[lessonId]?.completed ?? false
  }, [state.lessons])

  const getCompletedCount = useCallback(() => {
    return Object.values(state.lessons).filter((l) => l.completed).length
  }, [state.lessons])

  return {
    state,
    isLoaded,
    markComplete,
    saveNotes,
    toggleBookmark,
    setLastVisited,
    resetProgress,
    isCompleted,
    getCompletedCount,
    bookmarks: state.bookmarks,
    xp: state.xp,
    streak: state.streak,
  }
}

// Helper: progress for a specific module
export function getModuleProgress(state: ProgressState, lessonIds: string[]) {
  const completed = lessonIds.filter((id) => state.lessons[id]?.completed).length
  return {
    completed,
    total: lessonIds.length,
    percentage: lessonIds.length ? Math.round((completed / lessonIds.length) * 100) : 0,
  }
}

===== FILE: src/lib/admin.ts =====
/**
 * Maintainer access is controlled by a comma-separated ADMIN_EMAILS
 * environment variable. Keep this server-only; never expose it to clients.
 * The admin user (admin@nyxora.local) is always considered an admin.
 */
export function isAdmin(email: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  if (!email) return false

  // The dedicated admin account always has access
  if (email.toLowerCase() === 'admin@nyxora.local') return true

  const admins = (process.env.ADMIN_EMAILS || '')
    .split(',')
    .map((value) => value.trim().toLowerCase())
    .filter(Boolean)

  return admins.includes(email.trim().toLowerCase())
}


===== FILE: src/lib/auth/auth.ts =====
import NextAuth, { type NextAuthConfig, type Session } from 'next-auth'
import { PrismaAdapter } from '@auth/prisma-adapter'
import GitHub from 'next-auth/providers/github'
import Google from 'next-auth/providers/google'
import Email from 'next-auth/providers/email'
import Credentials from 'next-auth/providers/credentials'
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db'
import { sendVerificationRequest } from '@/lib/auth/email'
import { isAdmin } from '@/lib/admin'
import { getRequestIp, hashIp, isAdminLoginRateLimited, recordAdminLogin } from '@/lib/security'
import { compareSync } from 'bcrypt-ts'

const providers: NextAuthConfig['providers'] = []

if (process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID && process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET) {
  providers.push(
    GitHub({
      clientId: process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_ID,
      clientSecret: process.env.GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET,
    })
  )
}

if (process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID && process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET) {
  providers.push(
    Google({
      clientId: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID,
      clientSecret: process.env.GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET,
    })
  )
}

if (process.env.EMAIL_SERVER) {
  providers.push(
    Email({
      server: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER,
      from: process.env.EMAIL_FROM,
      sendVerificationRequest,
    })
  )
}

// Admin credentials provider
providers.push(
  Credentials({
    id: 'admin',
    name: 'Admin',
    credentials: {
      username: { label: 'Username', type: 'text' },
      password: { label: 'Password', type: 'password' },
    },
    async authorize(credentials, request) {
      if (!credentials?.username || !credentials?.password) return null

      const ipHash = hashIp(getRequestIp(request))

      try {
        if (await isAdminLoginRateLimited(ipHash)) {
          return null
        }

        const normalizedUsername = String(credentials.username).trim().toLowerCase()
        const adminUser = await prisma.user.findUnique({
          where: { email: 'admin@nyxora.local' },
          select: {
            id: true,
            name: true,
            email: true,
            image: true,
            passwordHash: true,
          },
        })

        if (
          !adminUser?.passwordHash ||
          !isAdmin(adminUser.email) ||
          normalizedUsername !== adminUser.email.toLowerCase()
        ) {
          await recordAdminLogin({ actorId: adminUser?.id, ipHash, success: false })
          return null
        }

        const isValid = compareSync(
          credentials.password as string,
          adminUser.passwordHash
        )

        if (!isValid) {
          await recordAdminLogin({ actorId: adminUser.id, ipHash, success: false })
          return null
        }

        await recordAdminLogin({ actorId: adminUser.id, ipHash, success: true })

        return {
          id: adminUser.id,
          name: adminUser.name ?? 'nyx',
          email: adminUser.email,
          image: adminUser.image,
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Admin credential check failed', error)
        return null
      }
    },
  })
)

const hasDatabase = Boolean(process.env.DATABASE_URL)

export const { handlers, auth, signIn, signOut } = NextAuth({
  trustHost: true,
  // Use the app's own auth screens instead of NextAuth's default /api/auth ones.
  pages: {
    signIn: '/auth/signin',
    error: '/auth/error',
    verifyRequest: '/auth/verify-request',
  },
  // Use JWT sessions for all providers. The Prisma adapter is still used for
  // account linking and user management when a database is configured.
  ...(hasDatabase ? { adapter: PrismaAdapter(prisma) } : {}),
  session: { strategy: 'jwt' as const, maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30 }, // 30 days
  providers,
  cookies: {
    csrfToken: {
      name: 'authjs.csrf-token',
      options: {
        httpOnly: true,
        sameSite: 'lax', // Changed from 'none' to 'lax' for better compatibility
        path: '/',
        secure: true,
      },
    },
    sessionToken: {
      name: '__Secure-authjs.session-token',
      options: {
        httpOnly: true,
        sameSite: 'lax',
        path: '/',
        secure: true,
        maxAge: 60 * 60 * 24 * 30, // 30 days
      },
    },
  },
  callbacks: {
    async session({ session, token }): Promise<Session> {
      if (session.user) {
        const user = {
          ...session.user,
          id: (token?.sub as string) ?? '',
          name: token?.name ?? null,
          email: token?.email ?? '',
          image: token?.picture ?? null,
          isAdmin: isAdmin(typeof token?.email === 'string' ? token.email : null),
        }
        session.user = user
      }
      return session
    },
    async jwt({ token, user, trigger, session }) {
      if (user) {
        token.sub = user.id
        if (user.name != null) token.name = user.name
        if (user.email != null) token.email = user.email
        if (user.image != null) token.picture = user.image
      }

      // Only sync with database on sign-in or when explicitly triggered
      // NOT on every request - this was a major performance bottleneck
      if (user) {
        // Initial sign-in: fetch fresh user data
        try {
          const dbUser = await prisma.user.findUnique({
            where: { id: token.sub },
            select: { name: true, image: true, email: true },
          })
          if (dbUser) {
            token.name = dbUser.name
            token.image = dbUser.image
            token.email = dbUser.email
          }
        } catch {
          // Ignore DB errors, keep existing token
        }
      }

      // Only sync on explicit update trigger
      if (trigger === 'update' && session && typeof session === 'object') {
        const updateData = session as { name?: string | null; image?: string | null }
        if (updateData.name != null) token.name = updateData.name
        if (updateData.image != null) token.picture = updateData.image
      }
      return token
    },
    async redirect({ url, baseUrl }) {
      if (url.startsWith('/')) return `${baseUrl}${url}`
      if (new URL(url).origin === baseUrl) return url
      return `${baseUrl}/learn`
    },
  },
  debug: process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development',
})


===== FILE: src/lib/auth/email.ts =====
export async function sendVerificationRequest(params: {
  identifier: string
  url: string
  provider: { from?: string | null }
}) {
  const { identifier: email, url, provider } = params
  const from = provider.from || process.env.EMAIL_FROM || 'Nyxora Sync <noreply@localhost>'

  const { default: nodemailer } = await import('nodemailer')

  const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
    host: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_HOST,
    port: Number(process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_PORT),
    auth: {
      user: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_USER,
      pass: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_PASSWORD,
    },
  })

  await transporter.sendMail({
    from,
    to: email,
    subject: `Sign in to Nyxora Sync`,
    text: `Sign in to Nyxora Sync\n${url}\n\nThis link expires in 24 hours.`,
    html: `
      <!DOCTYPE html>
      <html>
        <body style="font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, sans-serif; line-height: 1.6; color: #1a1a2e; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 20px;">
          <div style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, #1a1a2e 0%, #16213e 100%); border-radius: 16px; padding: 40px; text-align: center;">
            <h1 style="color: #fff; margin: 0 0 16px; font-size: 28px;">Nyxora Sync</h1>
            <p style="color: #a0aec0; margin: 0 0 32px;">Sign in to continue your learning journey</p>
            <a href="${url}" style="display: inline-block; background: #10b981; color: white; padding: 14px 32px; border-radius: 8px; text-decoration: none; font-weight: 600; font-size: 16px;">Sign in to Nyxora Sync</a>
            <p style="color: #718096; margin: 24px 0 0; font-size: 14px;">This link expires in 24 hours.<br>If you didn't request this, please ignore this email.</p>
          </div>
        </body>
      </html>
    `,
  })
}

===== FILE: src/lib/badge-awards.ts =====
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db'
import {
  badgeDefinitions,
  getEarnedBadges,
  tierOrder,
  type BadgeDefinition,
} from '@/lib/badges'
import type { ProgressState } from '@/lib/progress-state'

export interface EarnedBadge extends BadgeDefinition {
  earnedAt: string | null
}

type BadgeContextRow = Awaited<ReturnType<typeof loadBadgeData>>

/**
 * Loads the same progress/community inputs the client-side badge checks use.
 * Keeping this in one place means dashboard, profile and persistence never
 * drift apart.
 */
export async function loadBadgeData(userId: string) {
  const [user, stats, completedRows, projectCount, commentCount, upvoteCount, followerCount] =
    await Promise.all([
      prisma.user.findUnique({
        where: { id: userId },
        select: { createdAt: true },
      }),
      prisma.userStats.findUnique({
        where: { userId },
        select: { xp: true, streakCurrent: true, streakLongest: true },
      }),
      prisma.userProgress.findMany({
        where: { userId, completed: true },
        select: { lessonKey: true, completedAt: true },
      }),
      prisma.project.count({ where: { userId } }),
      prisma.comment.count({ where: { userId } }),
      prisma.projectUpvote.count({ where: { userId } }),
      prisma.follow.count({ where: { followingId: userId, status: 'accepted' } }),
    ])

  const state: ProgressState = {
    lessons: Object.fromEntries(
      completedRows.map((row) => [
        row.lessonKey,
        {
          completed: true,
          ...(row.completedAt ? { completedAt: row.completedAt.toISOString() } : {}),
        },
      ])
    ),
    xp: stats?.xp ?? 0,
    streak: {
      current: stats?.streakCurrent ?? 0,
      longest: stats?.streakLongest ?? 0,
      lastActiveDate: null,
    },
    bookmarks: [],
    lastVisited: null,
  }

  return {
    state,
    contributions: {
      projects: projectCount,
      comments: commentCount,
      upvotes: upvoteCount,
    },
    followers: followerCount,
    joinedAt: user?.createdAt.toISOString() ?? null,
  }
}

export function earnedFromData(data: BadgeContextRow): BadgeDefinition[] {
  return getEarnedBadges({
    state: data.state,
    contributions: data.contributions,
    followers: data.followers,
    joinedAt: data.joinedAt,
  })
}

/**
 * Materializes earned badges in UserBadge, prunes computed badges that no
 * longer qualify (for example after a progress reset), and keeps the profile
 * showcase limited to badges the user still owns.
 */
export async function syncUserBadges(userId: string): Promise<{
  earned: EarnedBadge[]
  newKeys: string[]
}> {
  const data = await loadBadgeData(userId)
  const earned = earnedFromData(data)
  const earnedKeys = new Set(earned.map((badge) => badge.key))

  const [allDefinitionsInDb, existing] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.badge.findMany({
      where: { key: { in: badgeDefinitions.map((badge) => badge.key) } },
      select: { id: true, key: true },
    }),
    prisma.userBadge.findMany({
      where: { userId },
      select: { badgeId: true, earnedAt: true, badge: { select: { key: true } } },
    }),
  ])

  const badgeIdByKey = new Map(allDefinitionsInDb.map((badge) => [badge.key, badge.id]))
  const missingDefinitions = earned.filter((badge) => !badgeIdByKey.has(badge.key))

  if (missingDefinitions.length > 0) {
    await prisma.badge.createMany({
      data: missingDefinitions.map((badge) => ({
        key: badge.key,
        name: badge.name,
        description: badge.description,
        icon: badge.icon,
        category: badge.category,
        criteria: {
          type: 'computed',
          tier: badge.tier,
        },
      })),
      skipDuplicates: true,
    })

    const refreshed = await prisma.badge.findMany({
      where: { key: { in: missingDefinitions.map((badge) => badge.key) } },
      select: { id: true, key: true },
    })
    for (const badge of refreshed) badgeIdByKey.set(badge.key, badge.id)
  }

  const earnedRows = earned
    .map((badge) => {
      const badgeId = badgeIdByKey.get(badge.key)
      return badgeId ? { userId, badgeId } : null
    })
    .filter((row): row is { userId: string; badgeId: string } => row !== null)

  const existingKeys = new Set(existing.map((row) => row.badge.key))
  const newKeys = earned.filter((badge) => !existingKeys.has(badge.key)).map((badge) => badge.key)

  if (earnedRows.length > 0) {
    await prisma.userBadge.createMany({
      data: earnedRows,
      skipDuplicates: true,
    })
  }

  // Remove computed badges that are no longer earned. Secret/manual awards are
  // not in badgeDefinitions and are therefore never touched.
  const computedIds = badgeDefinitions
    .map((badge) => badgeIdByKey.get(badge.key))
    .filter((id): id is string => Boolean(id))
  const earnedIds = earnedRows.map((row) => row.badgeId)

  if (computedIds.length > 0) {
    await prisma.userBadge.deleteMany({
      where: {
        userId,
        badgeId: {
          in: computedIds,
          ...(earnedIds.length > 0 ? { notIn: earnedIds } : {}),
        },
      },
    })
  }

  const preferences = await prisma.userPreferences.findUnique({
    where: { userId },
    select: { featuredBadges: true },
  })

  if (preferences) {
    const validFeatured = preferences.featuredBadges
      .filter((key) => earnedKeys.has(key))
      .slice(0, 3)

    if (validFeatured.length !== preferences.featuredBadges.length) {
      await prisma.userPreferences.update({
        where: { userId },
        data: { featuredBadges: validFeatured },
      })
    }
  }

  const earnedAtByKey = new Map(existing.map((row) => [row.badge.key, row.earnedAt.toISOString()]))

  return {
    earned: earned
      .map((badge) => ({
        ...badge,
        earnedAt: earnedAtByKey.get(badge.key) ?? null,
      }))
      .sort(
        (a, b) =>
          tierOrder[a.tier] - tierOrder[b.tier] ||
          badgeDefinitions.indexOf(a) - badgeDefinitions.indexOf(b)
      ),
    newKeys,
  }
}

/** Reads persisted badges without recomputing; used by the public profile. */
export async function getPersistedBadges(userId: string): Promise<EarnedBadge[]> {
  const rows = await prisma.userBadge.findMany({
    where: { userId },
    select: {
      earnedAt: true,
      badge: {
        select: {
          key: true,
          name: true,
          description: true,
          icon: true,
          category: true,
        },
      },
    },
    orderBy: { earnedAt: 'asc' },
  })

  const definitionByKey = new Map(badgeDefinitions.map((badge) => [badge.key, badge]))
  const badges: EarnedBadge[] = []

  for (const row of rows) {
    const definition = definitionByKey.get(row.badge.key)
    if (!definition) continue
    badges.push({
      ...definition,
      earnedAt: row.earnedAt.toISOString(),
    })
  }

  return badges.sort(
    (a, b) =>
      tierOrder[a.tier] - tierOrder[b.tier] ||
      badgeDefinitions.indexOf(a) - badgeDefinitions.indexOf(b)
  )
}


===== FILE: src/lib/badge-meta.ts =====
import type { ProgressState } from '@/lib/progress-state'

// Badge tier and category constants
export const tierLabels = {
  bronze: 'Bronze',
  silver: 'Silver',
  gold: 'Gold',
  platinum: 'Platinum',
  diamond: 'Diamond',
  legendary: 'Legendary',
} as const

export const tierIcons = {
  bronze: '🥉',
  silver: '🥈',
  gold: '🥇',
  platinum: '💎',
  diamond: '💠',
  legendary: '🏆',
} as const

export const tierColors = {
  bronze: 'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/5 text-amber-600',
  silver: 'border-slate-400/40 bg-slate-400/5 text-slate-600',
  gold: 'border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/5 text-amber-600',
  platinum: 'border-violet-400/40 bg-violet-400/5 text-violet-600',
  diamond: 'border-cyan-400/40 bg-cyan-400/5 text-cyan-600',
  legendary: 'border-emerald-500/40 bg-emerald-500/5 text-emerald-600',
} as const

export const tierOrder = {
  legendary: 0,
  diamond: 1,
  platinum: 2,
  gold: 3,
  silver: 4,
  bronze: 5,
} as const

export const categoryIcons = {
  milestone: '🏁',
  streak: '🔥',
  xp: '⚡',
  project: '🏗️',
  community: '👥',
  mastery: '🎓',
  special: '✨',
} as const

export type BadgeTier = keyof typeof tierLabels
export type BadgeCategory = keyof typeof categoryIcons

export interface CommunityStats {
  contributions: {
    projects: number
    comments: number
    upvotes: number
  }
  followers: number
  joinedAt: string | null
}

export interface BadgeContext extends CommunityStats {
  state: ProgressState
}

export interface BadgeDefinition {
  key: string
  name: string
  icon: string
  description: string
  check: (context: BadgeContext) => boolean
  category: BadgeCategory
  tier: BadgeTier
}

export const EMPTY_COMMUNITY_STATS: CommunityStats = {
  contributions: { projects: 0, comments: 0, upvotes: 0 },
  followers: 0,
  joinedAt: null,
}


===== FILE: src/lib/badges.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import {
  badgeDefinitions,
  getEarnedBadges,
  sortBadgesByEarned,
  tierOrder,
} from '@/lib/badges'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import type { BadgeContext } from '@/lib/badge-meta'

const firstLesson = tracks[0]!.modules[0]!.lessons[0]!

function context(overrides: Partial<BadgeContext> = {}): BadgeContext {
  return {
    state: {
      lessons: {},
      xp: 0,
      streak: { current: 0, longest: 0, lastActiveDate: null },
      bookmarks: [],
      lastVisited: null,
    },
    contributions: { projects: 0, comments: 0, upvotes: 0 },
    followers: 0,
    joinedAt: null,
    ...overrides,
  }
}

describe('badge catalog', () => {
  it('keeps all 79 badge keys unique', () => {
    const keys = badgeDefinitions.map((badge) => badge.key)
    expect(badgeDefinitions).toHaveLength(79)
    expect(new Set(keys).size).toBe(keys.length)
  })

  it('uses a known tier for every badge', () => {
    for (const badge of badgeDefinitions) {
      expect(tierOrder[badge.tier]).toBeTypeOf('number')
    }
  })

  it('unlocks First Steps after one completed lesson', () => {
    const earned = getEarnedBadges(
      context({
        state: {
          ...context().state,
          lessons: { [firstLesson.id]: { completed: true } },
        },
      })
    )
    expect(earned.map((badge) => badge.key)).toContain('first-lesson')
  })

  it('unlocks community badges from contribution counts', () => {
    const earned = getEarnedBadges(
      context({ contributions: { projects: 1, comments: 1, upvotes: 1 } })
    )
    const keys = earned.map((badge) => badge.key)
    expect(keys).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['first-project', 'first-comment', 'first-upvote']))
  })

  it('places earned badges before locked badges', () => {
    const earned = new Set(['xp-50', 'first-lesson'])
    const sorted = sortBadgesByEarned(badgeDefinitions, earned)
    const firstLocked = sorted.findIndex((badge) => !earned.has(badge.key))
    const lastEarned = sorted.reduce(
      (last, badge, index) => (earned.has(badge.key) ? index : last),
      -1
    )
    expect(lastEarned).toBeLessThan(firstLocked)
  })
})


===== FILE: src/lib/badges.ts =====
import type { ProgressState } from '@/lib/progress-state'
import { tracks, type Lesson } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { tierOrder, type BadgeContext, type BadgeDefinition } from '@/lib/badge-meta'

export * from '@/lib/badge-meta'

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Progress helpers shared by the badge checks
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

const allLessons = tracks.flatMap((track) =>
  track.modules.flatMap((module) => module.lessons)
)

const ALL_LESSON_IDS = Array.from(new Set(allLessons.map((lesson) => lesson.id)))

export const TOTAL_LESSON_COUNT = ALL_LESSON_IDS.length

const languageTrackKeys = tracks
  .filter((track) => track.category === 'programming-languages')
  .map((track) => track.key)

function completedCount(state: ProgressState): number {
  return Object.values(state.lessons).filter((lesson) => lesson.completed).length
}

function trackLessons(trackKey: string): Lesson[] {
  const track = tracks.find((candidate) => candidate.key === trackKey)
  return track ? track.modules.flatMap((module) => module.lessons) : []
}

function moduleLessons(moduleKey: string): Lesson[] {
  for (const track of tracks) {
    const trackModule = track.modules.find((candidate) => candidate.key === moduleKey)
    if (trackModule) return trackModule.lessons
  }
  return []
}

function isModuleComplete(
  state: ProgressState,
  moduleKey: string,
  matches: (lesson: Lesson) => boolean = () => true
): boolean {
  const lessons = moduleLessons(moduleKey).filter(matches)
  return lessons.length > 0 && lessons.every((lesson) => state.lessons[lesson.id]?.completed)
}

function isTrackComplete(state: ProgressState, trackKey: string): boolean {
  const lessons = trackLessons(trackKey)
  return lessons.length > 0 && lessons.every((lesson) => state.lessons[lesson.id]?.completed)
}

function isLanguageComplete(state: ProgressState, trackKey: string): boolean {
  const lessons = trackLessons(trackKey)
  return lessons.length > 0 && lessons.every((lesson) => state.lessons[lesson.id]?.completed)
}

function completedLanguageCount(state: ProgressState): number {
  return languageTrackKeys.filter((trackKey) =>
    trackLessons(trackKey).some((lesson) => state.lessons[lesson.id]?.completed)
  ).length
}

function completedDates(state: ProgressState): Date[] {
  return Object.values(state.lessons)
    .filter((lesson) => lesson.completed && lesson.completedAt)
    .map((lesson) => new Date(lesson.completedAt as string))
    .filter((date) => !Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))
}

function hasCompletionInHourRange(state: ProgressState, startHour: number, endHour: number): boolean {
  return completedDates(state).some((date) => {
    const hour = date.getHours()
    return hour >= startHour && hour < endHour
  })
}

function maxLessonsInOneDay(state: ProgressState): number {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>()
  for (const date of completedDates(state)) {
    const key = date.toDateString()
    counts.set(key, (counts.get(key) ?? 0) + 1)
  }
  return Math.max(0, ...counts.values())
}

function hasWeekendBothDays(state: ProgressState): boolean {
  const weekendDays = new Map<string, Set<number>>()

  for (const date of completedDates(state)) {
    const day = date.getDay()
    if (day !== 0 && day !== 6) continue

    // Key each weekend by its Monday so Saturday and Sunday line up.
    const monday = new Date(date)
    monday.setHours(0, 0, 0, 0)
    monday.setDate(date.getDate() - ((day + 6) % 7))
    const weekKey = monday.toDateString()

    const days = weekendDays.get(weekKey) ?? new Set<number>()
    days.add(day)
    weekendDays.set(weekKey, days)
  }

  return Array.from(weekendDays.values()).some((days) => days.has(0) && days.has(6))
}

export function getEarnedBadges(context: BadgeContext): BadgeDefinition[] {
  return badgeDefinitions.filter((badge) => badge.check(context))
}

export function sortBadgesByEarned(
  badges: BadgeDefinition[],
  earnedKeys: Set<string>
): BadgeDefinition[] {
  return [...badges].sort((a, b) => {
    const earnedA = earnedKeys.has(a.key) ? 0 : 1
    const earnedB = earnedKeys.has(b.key) ? 0 : 1
    if (earnedA !== earnedB) return earnedA - earnedB

    const tierA = tierOrder[a.tier]
    const tierB = tierOrder[b.tier]
    if (tierA !== tierB) return tierA - tierB

    return badges.indexOf(a) - badges.indexOf(b)
  })
}

/**
 * Shared wording for the certificate feature. Keep the disclaimer consistent
 * everywhere it appears so the site never implies accreditation.
 */
export const CERTIFICATE_DISCLAIMER =
  'Nyxora Sync is not a recognized school, university, or accrediting body. This certificate is an informal completion record for showcase only — it is not a certified, accredited, or official qualification and may hold no value in real life.'

export const CERTIFICATE_DISCLAIMER_SHORT =
  'Informal, non-accredited completion record for showcase only. Nyxora Sync is not a recognized institution, and this certificate may hold no value in real life.'

export const CERTIFICATE_ISSUANCE_NOTE =
  'Sign in to sync your progress. A certificate is issued automatically when every lesson in a track is complete.'

export const CERTIFICATE_VALUE_NOTE =
  'Treat it as a personal milestone and a showcase item, not as proof of formal education or a credential an employer is expected to recognize.'


===== FILE: src/lib/certificates.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { trackProgress } from '@/lib/certificates'

describe('trackProgress', () => {
  it('reports zero for an empty completed set', () => {
    const progress = trackProgress('beginner', new Set())
    expect(progress.completed).toBe(0)
    expect(progress.total).toBeGreaterThan(0)
    expect(progress.percent).toBe(0)
  })

  it('reports 100 percent when every lesson is complete', () => {
    const track = tracks.find((candidate) => candidate.key === 'beginner')!
    const completed = new Set(track.modules.flatMap((mod) => mod.lessons.map((lesson) => lesson.id)))
    const progress = trackProgress(track.key, completed)
    expect(progress.completed).toBe(progress.total)
    expect(progress.percent).toBe(100)
  })

  it('returns an empty result for an unknown track', () => {
    expect(trackProgress('missing-track', new Set())).toEqual({
      completed: 0,
      total: 0,
      percent: 0,
    })
  })
})


===== FILE: src/lib/certificates.ts =====
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'

export interface EarnedCertificate {
  id: string
  verificationId: string
  trackKey: string
  trackName: string
  recipientName: string | null
  issuedAt: string
}

function trackLessonIds(track: (typeof tracks)[number]): string[] {
  return track.modules.flatMap((mod) => mod.lessons.map((lesson) => lesson.id))
}

export function trackProgress(trackKey: string, completedLessonIds: Set<string>) {
  const track = tracks.find((candidate) => candidate.key === trackKey)
  if (!track) return { completed: 0, total: 0, percent: 0 }
  const lessonIds = trackLessonIds(track)
  const completed = lessonIds.filter((id) => completedLessonIds.has(id)).length
  return {
    completed,
    total: lessonIds.length,
    percent: lessonIds.length === 0 ? 0 : Math.round((completed / lessonIds.length) * 100),
  }
}

/**
 * Issue certificates for tracks whose every lesson is complete. Idempotent:
 * the unique (userId, trackKey) constraint and createMany skipDuplicates make
 * concurrent progress writes safe.
 */
export async function syncUserCertificates(userId: string): Promise<EarnedCertificate[]> {
  const [user, completedRows] = await Promise.all([
    prisma.user.findUnique({
      where: { id: userId },
      select: { name: true },
    }),
    prisma.userProgress.findMany({
      where: { userId, completed: true },
      select: { lessonKey: true },
    }),
  ])

  if (!user) return []

  const completedIds = new Set(completedRows.map((row) => row.lessonKey))
  const eligible = tracks.filter((track) => {
    const lessonIds = trackLessonIds(track)
    return lessonIds.length > 0 && lessonIds.every((id) => completedIds.has(id))
  })

  if (eligible.length > 0) {
    await prisma.certificate.createMany({
      data: eligible.map((track) => ({
        userId,
        trackKey: track.key,
        trackName: track.name,
        recipientName: user.name?.trim() || 'Nyxora Sync Learner',
      })),
      skipDuplicates: true,
    })
  }

  return getUserCertificates(userId)
}

export async function getUserCertificates(userId: string): Promise<EarnedCertificate[]> {
  const rows = await prisma.certificate.findMany({
    where: { userId },
    orderBy: { issuedAt: 'desc' },
    select: {
      id: true,
      verificationId: true,
      trackKey: true,
      trackName: true,
      recipientName: true,
      issuedAt: true,
    },
  })

  return rows.map((row) => ({
    ...row,
    issuedAt: row.issuedAt.toISOString(),
  }))
}

export async function getCertificateByVerificationId(verificationId: string) {
  return prisma.certificate.findUnique({
    where: { verificationId },
    select: {
      id: true,
      verificationId: true,
      trackKey: true,
      trackName: true,
      recipientName: true,
      issuedAt: true,
      user: { select: { name: true } },
    },
  })
}


===== FILE: src/lib/community-project.ts =====
/**
 * Shared shape for the project JSON returned by the showcase routes.
 * Keeping this in one place means create, list, detail, and edit responses
 * stay in sync as fields are added.
 */
export function serializeProject(
  project: {
    id: string
    title: string
    description: string
    githubUrl: string | null
    demoUrl: string | null
    imageUrl: string | null
    track: string
    modules: string[]
    tags: string[]
    upvotes: number
    createdAt: Date
    user: {
      id: string
      name: string | null
      image: string | null
      bio: string | null
      website: string | null
      githubUrl: string | null
      twitterUrl: string | null
      createdAt: Date
    }
    upvoteRows: { id: string }[]
  },
  commentCount = 0
) {
  return {
    id: project.id,
    title: project.title,
    description: project.description,
    githubUrl: project.githubUrl,
    demoUrl: project.demoUrl,
    imageUrl: project.imageUrl,
    track: project.track,
    modules: project.modules,
    tags: project.tags,
    upvotes: project.upvotes,
    commentCount,
    createdAt: project.createdAt.toISOString(),
    author: project.user,
    upvoted: project.upvoteRows.length > 0,
  }
}

===== FILE: src/lib/community.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { profileSchema } from '@/lib/community'

describe('profileSchema', () => {
  it('deduplicates featured badge keys', () => {
    const parsed = profileSchema.safeParse({
      featuredBadges: ['first-lesson', 'first-lesson', 'streak-1'],
    })
    expect(parsed.success).toBe(true)
    if (parsed.success) {
      expect(parsed.data.featuredBadges).toEqual(['first-lesson', 'streak-1'])
    }
  })

  it('rejects more than three pinned badges', () => {
    const parsed = profileSchema.safeParse({
      featuredBadges: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd'],
    })
    expect(parsed.success).toBe(false)
  })

  it('rejects unknown profile fields', () => {
    const parsed = profileSchema.safeParse({ isAdmin: true })
    expect(parsed.success).toBe(false)
  })
})


===== FILE: src/lib/community.ts =====
import { z } from 'zod'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'

/**
 * Community features (showcase, discussions, public profiles) all live in
 * PostgreSQL. The rest of the site keeps working without a database, so every
 * route checks this first and returns a friendly 503 instead of throwing.
 */
export function communityEnabled(): boolean {
  return Boolean(process.env.DATABASE_URL)
}

export function jsonError(status: number, code: string, message: string) {
  return Response.json(
    { error: { code, message } },
    { status, headers: { 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' } }
  )
}

export const COMMUNITY_DISABLED = {
  status: 503,
  code: 'COMMUNITY_UNAVAILABLE',
  message: 'Community features need a configured database. The lessons and playground still work.',
} as const

/** Fields safe to expose on a public profile. Never includes email. */
export const publicUserSelect = {
  id: true,
  name: true,
  image: true,
  bio: true,
  website: true,
  githubUrl: true,
  twitterUrl: true,
  createdAt: true,
} as const

const httpUrl = z
  .string()
  .trim()
  .max(2048)
  .url()
  .refine((value) => /^https?:\/\//i.test(value), 'Use an http(s) URL')

const optionalUrl = z
  .union([z.literal(''), httpUrl])
  .transform((value) => (value === '' ? null : value))
  .nullish()

const optionalText = (max: number) =>
  z
    .union([z.literal(''), z.string().trim().max(max)])
    .transform((value) => (value === '' ? null : value))
    .nullish()

const optionalName = z
  .union([z.literal(''), z.string().trim().min(1).max(80)])
  .transform((value) => (value === '' ? null : value))
  .nullish()

export const projectSchema = z
  .object({
    title: z.string().trim().min(4, 'Give your project a title').max(100),
    description: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .min(30, 'Describe what you built and what you learned (at least 30 characters)')
      .max(4000),
    githubUrl: optionalUrl,
    demoUrl: optionalUrl,
    imageUrl: optionalUrl,
    track: z
      .string()
      .trim()
      .refine((value) => tracks.some((track) => track.key === value), 'Choose a valid track'),
    modules: z.array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(60)).max(12).default([]),
    tags: z.array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(24)).max(8).default([]),
  })
  .strict()

export const projectUpdateSchema = projectSchema.partial().strict()

export const commentSchema = z
  .object({
    targetType: z.enum(['lesson', 'project']),
    targetId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(160),
    body: z.string().trim().min(2, 'Say a little more').max(2000),
    parentId: z.string().trim().min(1).max(40).nullish(),
  })
  .strict()

export const profileSchema = z
  .object({
    name: optionalName,
    bio: optionalText(600),
    website: optionalUrl,
    githubUrl: optionalUrl,
    twitterUrl: optionalUrl,
    showProgress: z.boolean().optional(),
    emailUpdates: z.boolean().optional(),
    featuredBadges: z
      .array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(160))
      .max(3, 'Pin at most 3 badges')
      .transform((keys) => Array.from(new Set(keys)))
      .optional(),
  })
  .strict()

/** All valid lesson ids, used to reject comments attached to made-up lessons. */
const lessonIds = new Set<string>()
for (const track of tracks) {
  for (const mod of track.modules) {
    for (const lesson of mod.lessons) {
      lessonIds.add(lesson.id)
    }
  }
}

export function isKnownLesson(id: string): boolean {
  return lessonIds.has(id)
}

export function getLimit(url: URL, fallback = 12, max = 48): number {
  const raw = Number(url.searchParams.get('limit'))
  if (!Number.isFinite(raw) || raw < 1) return fallback
  return Math.min(Math.floor(raw), max)
}

/** Basic sliding-window rate limit backed by row counts (no extra infra). */
export function sinceMinutes(minutes: number): Date {
  return new Date(Date.now() - minutes * 60 * 1000)
}

export function displayName(user: { name: string | null; email?: string | null }): string {
  return user.name?.trim() || user.email?.split('@')[0] || 'Anonymous learner'
}

export function isHttpUrl(value: string | null | undefined): value is string {
  return typeof value === 'string' && /^https?:\/\//i.test(value)
}


===== FILE: src/lib/contact-email.ts =====
interface ContactMessageInput {
  name: string | null
  email: string
  topic: string
  message: string
}

/**
 * Best-effort notification. The message is always stored first; a mail failure
 * must never turn a successfully stored message into an error for the sender.
 */
export async function sendContactNotification(input: ContactMessageInput): Promise<void> {
  const host = process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_HOST
  if (!host) return

  // Load nodemailer only when email is actually configured. Keeping it out of
  // the module graph lets the app run on Cloudflare Workers without SMTP.
  const { default: nodemailer } = await import('nodemailer')
  const port = Number(process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_PORT || 587)
  const transporter = nodemailer.createTransport({
    host,
    port,
    secure: port === 465,
    auth: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_USER
      ? {
          user: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_USER,
          pass: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_PASSWORD,
        }
      : undefined,
    connectionTimeout: 5000,
    greetingTimeout: 5000,
    socketTimeout: 10000,
  })

  const from = process.env.EMAIL_FROM || 'Nyxora Sync <noreply@localhost>'
  const to = process.env.CONTACT_INBOX || process.env.EMAIL_FROM || 'noreply@localhost'

  await transporter.sendMail({
    from,
    to,
    replyTo: input.email,
    subject: `[Nyxora Sync] ${input.topic} message`,
    text: [
      `From: ${input.name ?? 'Anonymous'} <${input.email}>`,
      `Topic: ${input.topic}`,
      '',
      input.message,
    ].join('\n'),
  })
}

    description: 'Learn Arduino, ESP32, and microcontroller programming. Build IoT devices, control sensors, and create hardware projects.',
    category: 'embedded-systems',
    icon: 'Cpu',
    color: 'amber',
    modules: [
      {
        id: 'embedded-coming-soon',
        key: 'coming-soon',
        name: 'Coming Soon',
        description: 'Arduino fundamentals, ESP32 WiFi/Bluetooth, sensors & actuators, RTOS, and IoT cloud integration.',
        order: 1,
        estimatedHours: 40,
        icon: 'Cpu',
        color: 'amber',
        lessons: [
          {
            id: 'embedded-placeholder',
            title: 'Track in Development',
            description: 'This learning path is being built. Check back soon for complete Arduino and ESP32 curricula.',
            order: 1,
            estimatedMinutes: 5,
            difficulty: 'beginner',
            xpReward: 0,
            keyTakeaways: ['Arduino basics', 'ESP32 WiFi/Bluetooth', 'Sensors & actuators', 'IoT cloud integration'],
            resources: [],
            isProject: false,
          },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
]

export function getTrack(key: string): Track | undefined {
  return tracks.find((t) => t.key === key)
}

export function getModule(trackKey: string, moduleKey: string): Module | undefined {
  const track = getTrack(trackKey)
  return track?.modules.find((m) => m.key === moduleKey)
}

export function getLesson(trackKey: string, moduleKey: string, lessonId: string): Lesson | undefined {
  const mod = getModule(trackKey, moduleKey)
  return mod?.lessons.find((l) => l.id === lessonId)
}

export function getAllLessons(trackKey: string): Lesson[] {
  const track = getTrack(trackKey)
  if (!track) return []
  return track.modules.flatMap((m) => m.lessons)
}

export function getTotalXP(trackKey: string): number {
  return getAllLessons(trackKey).reduce((sum, lesson) => sum + lesson.xpReward, 0)
}

export function getTotalMinutes(trackKey: string): number {
  return getAllLessons(trackKey).reduce((sum, lesson) => sum + lesson.estimatedMinutes, 0)
}

===== FILE: src/lib/db.ts =====
import { PrismaClient } from '@prisma/client'
import { PrismaPg } from '@prisma/adapter-pg'

// Vercel reuses a warm function instance for multiple requests, so a single
// Prisma client (and its pg pool) should be shared instead of reconnecting on
// every invocation. The module cache is enough in production; the global is
// for local development hot reloads.
const globalForPrisma = globalThis as unknown as {
  prisma: PrismaClient | undefined
}

function createPrismaClient() {
  const connectionString = process.env.DATABASE_URL

  if (connectionString) {
    // Use pooled connection for serverless (max 5 connections per instance)
    // The pooler URL should be used for the app, directUrl only for migrations
    return new PrismaClient({
      adapter: new PrismaPg({ connectionString }),
      log:
        process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'
          ? ['query', 'error', 'warn']
          : ['error'],
    })
  }

  // Local fallback when no database is configured. The app's core learning
  // experience works without auth/community persistence.
  return new PrismaClient({
    log:
      process.env.NODE_ENV === 'development'
        ? ['query', 'error', 'warn']
        : ['error'],
  })
}

export const prisma = globalForPrisma.prisma ?? createPrismaClient()

if (process.env.NODE_ENV !== 'production') {
  globalForPrisma.prisma = prisma
}

export default prisma

===== FILE: src/lib/language-curriculum.ts =====
import type { Difficulty, Lesson, Module, Resource, Track } from './curriculum'

/**
 * Language tracks are kept in their own module so the growing catalog stays
 * readable. They use the same Lesson/Module/Track shape as the web tracks, so
 * progress, search, sitemap, and lesson rendering work without special cases.
 */

const docs = (title: string, url: string, provider: string): Resource => ({
  type: 'docs',
  title,
  url,
  provider,
  isVerified: true,
})

const practice = (title: string, url: string, provider: string): Resource => ({
  type: 'interactive',
  title,
  url,
  provider,
  isVerified: true,
})

const lesson = (
  id: string,
  title: string,
  description: string,
  order: number,
  estimatedMinutes: number,
  difficulty: Difficulty,
  xpReward: number,
  keyTakeaways: string[],
  resources: Resource[]
): Lesson => ({
  id,
  title,
  description,
  order,
  estimatedMinutes,
  difficulty,
  xpReward,
  keyTakeaways,
  resources,
  isProject: id.includes('project') || id.includes('capstone'),
})

const moduleDef = (
  id: string,
  name: string,
  description: string,
  order: number,
  estimatedHours: number,
  color: string,
  lessons: Lesson[]
): Module => ({
  id,
  key: id,
  name,
  description,
  order,
  estimatedHours,
  icon: 'FileCode',
  color,
  lessons,
})

===== FILE: src/lib/lesson-links.test.ts =====
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest'
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { getLessonHref } from '@/lib/lesson-links'

const knownLesson = tracks[0]!.modules[0]!.lessons[0]!

describe('getLessonHref', () => {
  it('builds a path for a known lesson', () => {
    expect(getLessonHref(knownLesson.id)).toContain(knownLesson.id)
    expect(getLessonHref(knownLesson.id)).toMatch(/^\/learn\//)
  })

  it('falls back to the curriculum index for unknown ids', () => {
    expect(getLessonHref('not-a-real-lesson')).toBe('/learn')
  })
})


===== FILE: src/lib/lesson-links.ts =====
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { canonicalLearnPath } from '@/lib/seo'

/** Resolve a stable curriculum lesson id to the canonical lesson URL. */
export function getLessonHref(lessonId: string): string {
  for (const track of tracks) {
    for (const mod of track.modules) {
      if (mod.lessons.some((lesson) => lesson.id === lessonId)) {
        return canonicalLearnPath(track.key, mod.key, lessonId)
      }
    }
  }
  return '/learn'
}

export function formatLessonUpdated(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(`${value}T00:00:00Z`)
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return value
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-US', {
    dateStyle: 'medium',
    timeZone: 'UTC',
  }).format(date)
}


===== FILE: src/lib/newsletter-feature.ts =====
/**
 * Newsletter feature switch.
 *
 * The newsletter implementation is intentionally kept in the repository so it
 * can be re-enabled later. While this is `false`:
 *
 * - no newsletter form or link is rendered anywhere on the site
 * - `/newsletter`, `/newsletter/confirm`, and `/newsletter/unsubscribe` redirect home
 * - every newsletter and newsletter-admin API route returns 404
 * - the admin page returns 404
 *
 * The database models and any previously submitted addresses are retained.
 *
 * To re-enable:
 * 1. Set this to `true`.
 * 2. Configure SMTP and `ADMIN_EMAILS` in the deployment environment.
 * 3. Redeploy.
 *
 * The sitemap entry and public links follow this flag automatically.
 */
export const NEWSLETTER_ENABLED = false


===== FILE: src/lib/newsletter.ts =====
import type SMTPPool from 'nodemailer/lib/smtp-pool'
import type SMTPTransport from 'nodemailer/lib/smtp-transport'
import { isAdmin } from '@/lib/admin'

const DAY_SECONDS = 24 * 60 * 60
const CONFIRM_TTL_SECONDS = 2 * DAY_SECONDS
const UNSUBSCRIBE_TTL_SECONDS = 10 * 365 * DAY_SECONDS

export type NewsletterTokenPurpose = 'confirm' | 'unsubscribe'

interface TokenPayload {
  sub: string
  purpose: NewsletterTokenPurpose
  exp: number
  v: 1
}

function tokenSecret(): string | null {
  return (
    process.env.NEWSLETTER_SECRET ||
    process.env.AUTH_SECRET ||
    process.env.NEXTAUTH_SECRET ||
    null
  )
}

// Web Crypto API compatible HMAC (edge runtime compatible)
async function sign(value: string, secret: string): Promise<string> {
  const encoder = new TextEncoder()
  const key = await crypto.subtle.importKey(
    'raw',
    encoder.encode(secret),
    { name: 'HMAC', hash: 'SHA-256' },
    false,
    ['sign']
  )
  const signature = await crypto.subtle.sign('HMAC', key, encoder.encode(value))
  return btoa(String.fromCharCode(...new Uint8Array(signature)))
    .replace(/\+/g, '-')
    .replace(/\//g, '_')
    .replace(/=+$/, '')
}

function timingSafeEqual(a: Uint8Array, b: Uint8Array): boolean {
  if (a.length !== b.length) return false
  let diff = 0
  for (let i = 0; i < a.length; i++) {
    diff |= a[i] ^ b[i]
  }
  return diff === 0
}

export async function createNewsletterToken(
  subscriberId: string,
  purpose: NewsletterTokenPurpose
): Promise<string | null> {
  const secret = tokenSecret()
  if (!secret) return null

  const ttl = purpose === 'confirm' ? CONFIRM_TTL_SECONDS : UNSUBSCRIBE_TTL_SECONDS
  const payload: TokenPayload = {
    sub: subscriberId,
    purpose,
    exp: Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000) + ttl,
    v: 1,
  }
  const encoded = Buffer.from(JSON.stringify(payload), 'utf8').toString('base64url')
  const signature = await sign(encoded, secret)
  return `${encoded}.${signature}`
}

export async function verifyNewsletterToken(
  token: string,
  purpose: NewsletterTokenPurpose
): Promise<{ subscriberId: string } | null> {
  const secret = tokenSecret()
  if (!secret) return null

  const [encoded, signature, extra] = token.split('.')
  if (!encoded || !signature || extra) return null

  const expected = await sign(encoded, secret)
  const expectedBuffer = Buffer.from(expected)
  const providedBuffer = Buffer.from(signature)
  if (providedBuffer.length !== expectedBuffer.length || !timingSafeEqual(providedBuffer, expectedBuffer)) {
    return null
  }

  try {
    const parsed = JSON.parse(Buffer.from(encoded, 'base64url').toString('utf8')) as Partial<TokenPayload>
    if (
      parsed.v !== 1 ||
      parsed.purpose !== purpose ||
      typeof parsed.sub !== 'string' ||
      parsed.sub.length < 5 ||
      typeof parsed.exp !== 'number' ||
      parsed.exp <= Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000)
    ) {
      return null
    }
    return { subscriberId: parsed.sub }
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

interface SmtpConfig {
  host: string
  port: number
  secure: boolean
  auth?: { user: string; pass: string }
}

function smtpConfig(): SmtpConfig | null {
  if (process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_HOST) {
    const port = Number(process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_PORT || 587)
    return {
      host: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_HOST,
      port,
      secure: port === 465,
      ...(process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_USER
        ? {
            auth: {
              user: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_USER,
              pass: process.env.EMAIL_SERVER_PASSWORD || '',
            },
          }
        : {}),
    }
  }

  const server = process.env.EMAIL_SERVER
  if (!server) return null

  try {
    const url = new URL(server)
    const port = Number(url.port || (url.protocol === 'smtps:' ? 465 : 587))
    return {
      host: url.hostname,
      port,
      secure: url.protocol === 'smtps:' || port === 465,
      ...(url.username
        ? {
            auth: {
              user: decodeURIComponent(url.username),
              pass: decodeURIComponent(url.password),
            },
          }
        : {}),
    }
  } catch {
    return null
  }
}

export function newsletterEmailConfigured(): boolean {
  return Boolean(smtpConfig() && process.env.EMAIL_FROM)
}

export function isNewsletterAdmin(email: string | null | undefined): boolean {
  return isAdmin(email)
}

function fromAddress(): string {
  return process.env.EMAIL_FROM || 'Nyxora Sync <noreply@localhost>'
}

async function createTransport(pool = false) {
  const config = smtpConfig()
  if (!config) throw new Error('Email is not configured')

  // Loaded lazily so the Worker bundle stays free of SMTP code unless the
  // newsletter feature is actually configured.
  const { default: nodemailer } = await import('nodemailer')

  if (pool) {
    const options: SMTPPool.Options = {
      ...config,
      pool: true,
      maxConnections: 3,
      maxMessages: 50,
      connectionTimeout: 10_000,
      greetingTimeout: 10_000,
      socketTimeout: 20_000,
    }
    return nodemailer.createTransport(options)
  }

  const options: SMTPTransport.Options = {
    ...config,
    connectionTimeout: 10_000,
    greetingTimeout: 10_000,
    socketTimeout: 20_000,
  }
  return nodemailer.createTransport(options)
}

function escapeHtml(value: string): string {
  return value
    .replaceAll('&', '&amp;')
    .replaceAll('<', '&lt;')
    .replaceAll('>', '&gt;')
    .replaceAll('"', '&quot;')
    .replaceAll("'", '&#039;')
}

function actionUrl(origin: string, path: string, token: string): string {
  const url = new URL(path, origin)
  url.searchParams.set('token', token)
  return url.toString()
}

function emailShell({
  heading,
  bodyHtml,
  footerHtml,
}: {
  heading: string
  bodyHtml: string
  footerHtml: string
}): string {
  return `<!doctype html>
<html lang="en">
  <body style="margin:0;padding:0;background:#f4f4f5;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',Roboto,Helvetica,Arial,sans-serif;color:#18181b;">
    <div style="max-width:600px;margin:0 auto;padding:32px 16px;">
      <div style="background:#ffffff;border:1px solid #e4e4e7;border-radius:16px;overflow:hidden;">
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#064e3b,#4c1d95);padding:22px 28px;">
          <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:#ffffff;letter-spacing:-0.02em;">Nyxora Sync</div>
        </div>
        <div style="padding:30px 28px;">
          <h1 style="margin:0 0 18px;font-size:24px;line-height:1.3;color:#18181b;">${escapeHtml(heading)}</h1>
          ${bodyHtml}
        </div>
        <div style="padding:18px 28px;border-top:1px solid #e4e4e7;background:#fafafa;color:#71717a;font-size:12px;line-height:1.6;">
          ${footerHtml}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </body>
</html>`
}

export async function sendNewsletterConfirmation({
  email,
  subscriberId,
  origin,
}: {
  email: string
  subscriberId: string
  origin: string
}): Promise<void> {
  const token = await createNewsletterToken(subscriberId, 'confirm')
  if (!token) throw new Error('Newsletter token secret is not configured')

  const confirmUrl = actionUrl(origin, '/newsletter/confirm', token)
  const transport = await createTransport()

  try {
    await transport.sendMail({
      from: fromAddress(),
      to: email,
      subject: 'Confirm your Nyxora Sync newsletter subscription',
      text: [
        'Confirm your Nyxora Sync newsletter subscription',
        '',
        `Open this link to confirm: ${confirmUrl}`,
        '',
        'The link expires in 48 hours. If you did not sign up, ignore this email and nothing will be sent to you.',
      ].join('\n'),
      html: emailShell({
        heading: 'Confirm your subscription',
        bodyHtml: `<p style="margin:0 0 16px;font-size:15px;line-height:1.7;color:#3f3f46;">You asked to receive occasional Nyxora Sync curriculum updates. Confirm your address to join the list.</p>
          <p style="margin:0 0 24px;"><a href="${escapeHtml(confirmUrl)}" style="display:inline-block;background:#059669;color:#ffffff;text-decoration:none;font-weight:600;padding:12px 20px;border-radius:8px;">Confirm subscription</a></p>
          <p style="margin:0;font-size:13px;line-height:1.6;color:#71717a;">This link expires in 48 hours. If you did not sign up, ignore this email and nothing will be sent to you.</p>`,
        footerHtml: 'You received this because someone entered this address on mern-learn. If that was not you, no action is needed.',
      }),
    })
  } finally {
    transport.close()
  }
}

export interface NewsletterRecipient {
  id: string
  email: string
}

export async function sendNewsletterBroadcast({
  subject,
  body,
  recipients,
  origin,
  testTo,
}: {
  subject: string
  body: string
  recipients: NewsletterRecipient[]
  origin: string
  testTo?: string
}): Promise<{ sent: number; failed: number }> {
  const transport = await createTransport(true)
  const htmlBody = `<div style="margin:0;font-size:15px;line-height:1.75;color:#3f3f46;white-space:pre-wrap;">${escapeHtml(body)}</div>`

  async function sendOne(
    email: string,
    unsubscribeUrl: string,
    isTest: boolean,
    oneClickUrl?: string
  ) {
    const finalSubject = isTest ? `[TEST] ${subject}` : subject
    const footerText = isTest
      ? 'This is a test send from the Nyxora Sync newsletter admin page.'
      : 'You are receiving this because you confirmed your subscription to Nyxora Sync updates.'

    await transport.sendMail({
      from: fromAddress(),
      to: email,
      replyTo: process.env.CONTACT_INBOX || undefined,
      subject: finalSubject,
      text: [
        body,
        '',
        '---',
        footerText,
        `Unsubscribe: ${unsubscribeUrl}`,
        'Unsubscribing deletes your address from the list.',
      ].join('\n'),
      html: emailShell({
        heading: subject,
        bodyHtml: htmlBody,
        footerHtml: `${escapeHtml(footerText)}<br><a href="${escapeHtml(unsubscribeUrl)}" style="color:#4f46e5;">Unsubscribe</a> — this deletes your address from the list.`,
      }),
      ...(isTest || !oneClickUrl
        ? {}
        : {
            headers: {
              'List-Unsubscribe': `<${oneClickUrl}>`,
              'List-Unsubscribe-Post': 'List-Unsubscribe=One-Click',
              Precedence: 'bulk',
              'X-Auto-Response-Suppress': 'OOF',
            },
          }),
    })
  }

  try {
    if (testTo) {
      await sendOne(testTo, new URL('/newsletter', origin).toString(), true)
      return { sent: 1, failed: 0 }
    }

    let sent = 0
    let failed = 0

    // Keep concurrency modest so small SMTP providers do not throttle the send.
    for (let index = 0; index < recipients.length; index += 5) {
      const batch = recipients.slice(index, index + 5)
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(
        batch.map(async (recipient) => {
          const token = await createNewsletterToken(recipient.id, 'unsubscribe')
          if (!token) throw new Error('Newsletter token secret is not configured')
          return sendOne(
            recipient.email,
            actionUrl(origin, '/newsletter/unsubscribe', token),
            false,
            actionUrl(origin, '/api/newsletter/unsubscribe', token)
          )
        })
      )
      for (const result of results) {
        if (result.status === 'fulfilled') sent += 1
        else failed += 1
      }
    }

    return { sent, failed }
  } finally {
    transport.close()
  }
}


===== FILE: src/lib/progress-state.ts =====
// Shared progress state helpers used by the localStorage hook and the
// account-sync layer. This module is safe to import from client components
// (all browser access is guarded) and from route handlers for type sharing.

export interface LessonProgress {
  completed: boolean
  completedAt?: string
  notes?: string
}

export interface ProgressState {
  lessons: Record<string, LessonProgress> // lessonId -> progress
  xp: number
  streak: {
    current: number
    lastActiveDate: string | null
    longest: number
  }
  bookmarks: string[]
  lastVisited: {
    track: string
    module: string
    lesson: string
    timestamp: string
  } | null
}

export const GUEST_STORAGE_KEY = 'mern-learn-progress'
const USER_STORAGE_PREFIX = 'mern-learn-progress:u:'

export const defaultProgressState: ProgressState = {
  lessons: {},
  xp: 0,
  streak: {
    current: 0,
    lastActiveDate: null,
    longest: 0,
  },
  bookmarks: [],
  lastVisited: null,
}

// Which bucket is currently active. Set to the signed-in user's id by
// <ProgressSync>, so `useProgress` reads and writes the right bucket.
let activeUserId: string | null = null

export function getProgressStorageKey(userId: string | null = activeUserId): string {
  return userId ? `${USER_STORAGE_PREFIX}${userId}` : GUEST_STORAGE_KEY
}

export function getToday(): string {
  return new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]
}

export function loadProgressState(userId: string | null = activeUserId): ProgressState {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return defaultProgressState
  try {
    const stored = localStorage.getItem(getProgressStorageKey(userId))
    if (!stored) return defaultProgressState
    const parsed = JSON.parse(stored) as Partial<ProgressState>
    return {
      ...defaultProgressState,
      ...parsed,
      streak: { ...defaultProgressState.streak, ...parsed.streak },
    }
  } catch {
    return defaultProgressState
  }
}

export function broadcastProgressState(state: ProgressState) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('progress-updated', { detail: state }))
}

export function saveProgressState(state: ProgressState, userId: string | null = activeUserId) {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined') return
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(getProgressStorageKey(userId), JSON.stringify(state))
    broadcastProgressState(state)
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to save progress:', e)
  }
}

/**
 * Switch the active storage bucket. Called after sign-in resolves so progress
 * is scoped per account instead of leaking between users on a shared browser.
 */
export function switchProgressScope(userId: string | null) {
  activeUserId = userId
  broadcastProgressState(loadProgressState(userId))
}

function earlierDate(a?: string, b?: string): string | undefined {
  if (!a) return b
  if (!b) return a
  return a < b ? a : b
}

function mergeStreaks(a: ProgressState['streak'], b: ProgressState['streak']): ProgressState['streak'] {
  const aDate = a.lastActiveDate ?? ''
  const bDate = b.lastActiveDate ?? ''
  // Keep the most recently active streak, but never lose the best run.
  const latest = aDate >= bDate ? a : b
  return {
    current: latest.current,
    lastActiveDate: latest.lastActiveDate ?? null,
    longest: Math.max(a.longest, b.longest, a.current, b.current),
  }
}

/**
 * Merge two progress snapshots without losing work.
 * `primary` wins note conflicts; completion, bookmarks, and streaks are unioned.
 */
export function mergeProgressStates(primary: ProgressState, secondary: ProgressState): ProgressState {
  const lessons: Record<string, LessonProgress> = {}
  const ids = new Set([...Object.keys(primary.lessons), ...Object.keys(secondary.lessons)])

  for (const id of ids) {
    const a = primary.lessons[id]
    const b = secondary.lessons[id]
    const completed = Boolean(a?.completed || b?.completed)
    const notes = a?.notes?.trim() ? a.notes : b?.notes
    lessons[id] = {
      completed,
      completedAt: completed ? earlierDate(a?.completedAt, b?.completedAt) : undefined,
      ...(notes ? { notes } : {}),
    }
  }

  const bookmarks = Array.from(new Set([...primary.bookmarks, ...secondary.bookmarks]))

  let lastVisited = primary.lastVisited
  if (
    secondary.lastVisited &&
    (!lastVisited || secondary.lastVisited.timestamp > lastVisited.timestamp)
  ) {
    lastVisited = secondary.lastVisited
  }

  return {
    lessons,
    xp: Math.max(primary.xp, secondary.xp),
    streak: mergeStreaks(primary.streak, secondary.streak),
    bookmarks,
    lastVisited,
  }
}


===== FILE: src/lib/realtime/notify.ts =====
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db'
import { getLessonHref } from '@/lib/lesson-links'

function actorLabel(name: string | null | undefined): string {
  return name?.trim() || 'A learner'
}

/** Notify a project owner when someone comments on their project. */
export async function notifyCommentCreated(
  commentId: string,
  targetType: 'lesson' | 'project',
  targetId: string,
  authorId: string
): Promise<void> {
  if (targetType === 'lesson') return

  const project = await prisma.project.findUnique({
    where: { id: targetId },
    select: { userId: true },
  })

  if (!project || project.userId === authorId) return

  await prisma.notification.create({
    data: {
      userId: project.userId,
      actorId: authorId,
      type: 'comment_created',
      message: 'commented on your project',
      href: `/community/showcase/${targetId}?comment=${commentId}#discussion-heading`,
    },
  })
}

/** Notify the parent comment author about a reply. */
export async function notifyCommentReply(
  replyId: string,
  parentCommentId: string,
  authorId: string
): Promise<void> {
  const parentComment = await prisma.comment.findUnique({
    where: { id: parentCommentId },
    select: { userId: true, targetType: true, targetId: true },
  })

  if (!parentComment || parentComment.userId === authorId) return

  const href =
    parentComment.targetType === 'lesson'
      ? `${getLessonHref(parentComment.targetId)}?comment=${replyId}#discussion-heading`
      : `/community/showcase/${parentComment.targetId}?comment=${replyId}#discussion-heading`

  await prisma.notification.create({
    data: {
      userId: parentComment.userId,
      actorId: authorId,
      type: 'comment_reply',
      message: 'replied to your comment',
      href,
    },
  })
}

/** Notify someone that they received a follow request. */
export async function notifyFollowRequest(
  followerId: string,
  followingId: string
): Promise<void> {
  if (followerId === followingId) return

  const actor = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: followerId },
    select: { name: true },
  })

  await prisma.notification.create({
    data: {
      userId: followingId,
      actorId: followerId,
      type: 'follow_request',
      message: `${actorLabel(actor?.name)} sent you a follow request.`,
      href: '/settings?tab=profile',
    },
  })
}

/** Notify the requester that their follow request was accepted. */
export async function notifyFollowAccepted(
  followerId: string,
  followingId: string
): Promise<void> {
  const actor = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: followingId },
    select: { name: true },
  })

  await prisma.notification.create({
    data: {
      userId: followerId,
      actorId: followingId,
      type: 'follow_accepted',
      message: `${actorLabel(actor?.name)} accepted your follow request.`,
      href: `/community/people/${followingId}`,
    },
  })
}

/** Notify the requester that their follow request was declined. */
export async function notifyFollowRejected(
  followerId: string,
  followingId: string
): Promise<void> {
  const actor = await prisma.user.findUnique({
    where: { id: followingId },
    select: { name: true },
  })

  await prisma.notification.create({
    data: {
      userId: followerId,
      actorId: followingId,
      type: 'follow_rejected',
      message: `${actorLabel(actor?.name)} declined your follow request.`,
      href: `/community/people/${followingId}`,
    },
  })
}

/** Notify a project owner about a new upvote. */
export async function notifyProjectUpvote(
  projectId: string,
  voterId: string
): Promise<void> {
  const project = await prisma.project.findUnique({
    where: { id: projectId },
    select: { userId: true },
  })

  if (!project || project.userId === voterId) return

  await prisma.notification.create({
    data: {
      userId: project.userId,
      actorId: voterId,
      type: 'project_upvote',
      message: 'upvoted your project',
      href: `/community/showcase/${projectId}`,
    },
  })
}

===== FILE: src/lib/search.ts =====
import { tracks } from '@/lib/curriculum'
import { resourcesData } from '@/lib/resources-data'
import { faqSections } from '@/lib/faq'
import { canonicalLearnPath } from '@/lib/seo'
import { communityEnabled } from '@/lib/community'
import { prisma } from '@/lib/db'

export type SearchResultType =
  | 'page'
  | 'track'
  | 'module'
  | 'lesson'
  | 'resource'
  | 'faq'
  | 'project'

export interface SearchResult {
  id: string
  type: SearchResultType
  title: string
  description: string
  href: string
  meta?: string
  external?: boolean
  keywords?: string
}
